	<!-- Webflow Optimize tracking -->
	<script>
	(function () {
	'use strict';
	
	// Guard: only one instance
	if (window.__wfOptimizeTrackingInit) return;
	window.__wfOptimizeTrackingInit = true;
	
	// Ensure dataLayer exists (GTM may not have loaded yet)
	window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
	
	function pushVariation(result) {
	var formattedName = result.experienceName + ' (' + result.experienceType + ')';
	
	window.dataLayer.push({
	event: 'optimize_variation_recorded',
	optimize: {
	experienceId: result.experienceId,
	experienceName: result.experienceName,
	experienceType: result.experienceType,
	variationId: result.variationId,
	variationName: result.variationName,
	ccStatus: result.ccStatus || 'n/a',
	formattedName: formattedName,
	},
	});
	}
	
	// Register callback as early as possible
	if (typeof wf !== 'undefined' && typeof wf.ready === 'function') {
	wf.ready(function () {
	wf.onVariationRecorded(pushVariation);
	});
	} else {
	// wf not available yet — poll briefly
	// This script runs in <head> so wf should appear soon
	var attempts = 0;
	var poll = setInterval(function () {
	attempts++;
	if (typeof wf !== 'undefined' && typeof wf.ready === 'function') {
	clearInterval(poll);
	wf.ready(function () {
	wf.onVariationRecorded(pushVariation);
	});
	} else if (attempts > 20) {
	// 20 * 100ms = 2s — give up
	clearInterval(poll);
	}
	}, 100);
	}
	})();
	</script>
	
	<!-- Webflow Optimize refresh -->
	<script>
	window.__wfVariationRefreshQueue = window.__wfVariationRefreshQueue || [];
	window.__wfVariationRefreshScheduled = false;
	
	window.__registerWfVariationRefresh = function (fn) {
	if (typeof fn !== 'function') return;
	window.__wfVariationRefreshQueue.push(fn);
	};
	
	window.__runWfVariationRefreshes = function () {
	window.__wfVariationRefreshQueue.forEach(function (fn) {
	try {
	fn();
	} catch (e) {}
	});
	};
	
	window.__scheduleWfVariationRefreshes = function () {
	if (window.__wfVariationRefreshScheduled) return;
	window.__wfVariationRefreshScheduled = true;
	requestAnimationFrame(function () {
	requestAnimationFrame(function () {
	window.__wfVariationRefreshScheduled = false;
	window.__runWfVariationRefreshes();
	});
	});
	};
	
	if (window.wf && typeof window.wf.ready === 'function' && typeof window.wf.onVariationRecorded === 'function') {
	window.wf.ready(function () {
	window.wf.onVariationRecorded(function () {
	window.__scheduleWfVariationRefreshes();
	});
	});
	}
	
	// Fallback: catch cases where onVariationRecorded fires before consumers
	// register, or where the Webflow Optimize API is unavailable (e.g. preview mode)
	setTimeout(function () {
	if (window.__wfVariationRefreshQueue.length) {
	window.__runWfVariationRefreshes();
	}
	}, 500);
	</script><meta name="robots" content="noindex, follow"></head><body><div class="page_wrap"><div class="u-position-fixed"><div class="w-embed"><style>
	:root {
	--grid-breakout: [full-start] minmax(0, 1fr) [content-start] repeat(var(--site--column-count), minmax(0, var(--site--column-width))) [content-end] minmax(0, 1fr) [full-end];

	}
	::before,

	.w-richtext:before,
	.w-richtext:after {
	content: unset;
	}
	html {
	background-color: var(--_theme---background);
	}
	button {
	background-color: unset;
	padding: unset;
	text-align: inherit;
	}
	button:not(:disabled) {
	cursor: pointer;
	}
	video {
	width: 100%;
	object-fit: cover;
	}
	/* remove padding of empty element */
	.wf-empty {
	padding: 0;
	}
	svg {
	max-width: 100%;
	}
	.nav_logo {
	max-width: none !important;
	}
	@media (prefers-color-scheme: light) {
	option {
	color: black;
	}
	}
	img::selection {
	background: transparent;
	}
	/* Typography */
	body {
	text-transform: var(--_text-style---text-transform);
	font-smoothing: antialiased;
	-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
	}
	/* Clear Defaults */
	a:not([class]) {
	text-decoration: underline;
	}
	h1,
	h2,
	h3,
	h4,
	h5,
	h6,
	p,
	blockquote,
	label {
	font-family: inherit;
	font-size: inherit;
	font-weight: inherit;
	line-height: inherit;
	letter-spacing: inherit;
	text-transform: inherit;
	text-wrap: inherit;
	margin-top: 0;
	margin-bottom: 0;
	}
	select:has(option[value='']:checked) {
	color: color-mix(in lab, currentcolor 60%, transparent);
	}
	/* Selection Color */
	::selection {
	background-color: var(--_theme---selection--background);
	color: var(--_theme---selection--text);
	}
	/* Margin Trim */
	:is(.u-margin-trim, .u-rich-text) > :not(:not(.w-condition-invisible, .u-cover-absolute, .u-ignore-trim) ~ :not(.w-condition-invisible, .u-cover-absolute, .u-ignore-trim)),
	:is(.u-margin-trim, .u-rich-text) > :not(:not(.w-condition-invisible, .u-cover-absolute, .u-ignore-trim) ~ :not(.w-condition-invisible, .u-cover-absolute, .u-ignore-trim)).u-display-contents > :first-child {
	margin-top: 0;
	}
	:is(.u-margin-trim, .u-rich-text) > :not(:has(~ :not(.w-condition-invisible, .u-cover-absolute, .u-ignore-trim))),
	:is(.u-margin-trim, .u-rich-text) > :not(:has(~ :not(.w-condition-invisible, .u-cover-absolute, .u-ignore-trim))).u-display-contents > :last-child {
	margin-bottom: 0;

	/* Rich Text Links */
	.w-richtext a {
	position: relative;
	z-index: 4;
	}
	/* Line Clamp */
	.u-line-clamp-1,
	.u-line-clamp-2,
	.u-line-clamp-3,
	.u-line-clamp-4 {
	-webkit-line-clamp: 1;

	.u-line-clamp-3 {

	/* Child Contain */
	.u-child-contain > * {

	/* Hide */

	/* Focus State */
	a,

	[data-outline],

	outline-offset: var(--focus--offset-outer);
	}
	a:focus-visible,

	outline-color: color-mix(in lab, currentcolor 30%, transparent) !important;
	outline-width: var(--focus--width) !important;
	outline-style: solid !important;
	}
	/* Disabled */
	[data-button]:has(button:disabled) {
	opacity: 0.5;
	pointer-events: none;
	}

	z-index: 0;
	}

	display: none;

	}

	}
	.u-grid-above {
	display: flex !important;
	}
	}
	/* State Manager */
	[data-state] {
	--_state---true: 1;
	--_state---false: 0;
	}
	.is-active,
	[data-state~='checked']:is(:checked, :has(:checked)),
	[data-state~='current']:is(.w--current, :has(.w--current)),
	[data-state~='open']:is(.w--open, :has(.w--open)),
	[data-state~='pressed']:is([aria-pressed='true'], :has([aria-pressed='true'])),
	[data-state~='expanded']:is([aria-expanded='true'], :has([aria-expanded='true'])),
	[data-state~='external']:is([target='_blank'], :has([target='_blank'])) {
	--_state---true: 0;
	--_state---false: 1;
	}
	.form_ui_label[data-state~='checked']:has(.form_ui_input:checked) {
	background-color: var(--_theme---background-o5);
	border-color: transparent;
	}
	
	.form_ui_icon_wrap.is-plus {
	transition: transform 0.25s var(--ease);
	}
	.form_ui_label[data-state~='checked']:has(.form_ui_input:checked) .form_ui_icon_wrap.is-plus {

	[data-trigger~='group']:has([data-trigger~='focus-other']:focus-visible, [data-trigger~='focus-other'] :focus-visible) [data-trigger~='focus-other']:not(:focus-visible, :has(:focus-visible)) {

	[data-number="50"]{--number:50}[data-number="51"]{--number:51}[data-number="52"]{--number:52}[data-number="53"]{--number:53}[data-number="54"]{--number:54}[data-number="55"]{--number:55}[data-number="56"]{--number:56}[data-number="57"]{--number:57}[data-number="58"]{--number:58}[data-number="59"]{--number:59}

	[data-number="80"]{--number:80}[data-number="81"]{--number:81}[data-number="82"]{--number:82}[data-number="83"]{--number:83}[data-number="84"]{--number:84}[data-number="85"]{--number:85}[data-number="86"]{--number:86}[data-number="87"]{--number:87}[data-number="88"]{--number:88}[data-number="89"]{--number:89}
	[data-number="90"]{--number:90}[data-number="91"]{--number:91}[data-number="92"]{--number:92}[data-number="93"]{--number:93}[data-number="94"]{--number:94}[data-number="95"]{--number:95}[data-number="96"]{--number:96}[data-number="97"]{--number:97}[data-number="98"]{--number:98}[data-number="99"]{--number:99}[data-number="100"]{--number:100}
	
	/* Responsive Styles */
	[data-large-columns="1"] { display: flex; }
	[data-large-columns="2"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 2; }
	[data-large-columns="3"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 3; }
	[data-large-columns="4"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 4; }
	[data-large-columns="5"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 5; }
	[data-large-columns="6"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 6; }
	[data-large-columns="7"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 7; }
	[data-large-columns="8"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 8; }
	[data-large-columns="9"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 9; }
	[data-large-columns="10"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 10; }
	[data-large-columns="11"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 11; }
	[data-large-columns="12"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 12; }
	.slider_offset { --slide-count: var(--lg); --lg: 3; --md: var(--lg); --sm: var(--md); --xs: var(--sm); }
	
	@media screen and (width < 992px) {
	* {
	--_responsive---large: 0;
	--_responsive---medium: 1;
	--flex-medium: flex;
	--none-medium: none;
	--column-medium: column;
	--row-medium: row;
	--start-medium: start;
	--center-medium: center;
	--end-medium: end;
	--relative-medium: relative;
	}
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-medium-columns="1"] { display: flex; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-medium-columns="2"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 2; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-medium-columns="3"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 3; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-medium-columns="4"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 4; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-medium-columns="5"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 5; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-medium-columns="6"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 6; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-medium-columns="7"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 7; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-medium-columns="8"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 8; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-medium-columns="9"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 9; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-medium-columns="10"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 10; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-medium-columns="11"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 11; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-medium-columns="12"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 12; }
	.u-order-first-medium { order: -1; }
	.u-order-auto-medium { order: 0; }
	.u-order-last-medium { order: 1; }
	.u-display-contents-medium { display: contents; }
	.u-display-block-medium { display: block; }
	.u-display-grid-medium { display: grid; }
	.u-display-flex-medium { display: flex; }
	.u-display-none-medium { display: none; }
	.u-all-unset-medium { all: unset; }
	.slider_offset { --slide-count: var(--md); }
	}
	@media screen and (width < 768px) {
	* {
	--_responsive---medium: 0;
	--_responsive---small: 1;
	--flex-small: flex;
	--none-small: none;
	--column-small: column;
	--row-small: row;
	--start-small: start;
	--center-small: center;
	--end-small: end;
	--relative-small: relative;
	}
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-small-columns="1"] { display: flex; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-small-columns="2"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 2; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-small-columns="3"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 3; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-small-columns="4"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 4; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-small-columns="5"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 5; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-small-columns="6"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 6; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-small-columns="7"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 7; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-small-columns="8"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 8; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-small-columns="9"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 9; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-small-columns="10"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 10; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-small-columns="11"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 11; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-small-columns="12"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 12; }
	.u-order-first-small { order: -1; }
	.u-order-auto-small { order: 0; }
	.u-order-last-small { order: 1; }
	.u-display-contents-small { display: contents; }
	.u-display-block-small { display: block; }
	.u-display-grid-small { display: grid; }
	.u-display-flex-small { display: flex; }
	.u-display-none-small { display: none; }
	.u-all-unset-small { all: unset; }
	.slider_offset { --slide-count: var(--sm); }
	}
	@media screen and (width < 480px) {
	* {
	--_responsive---small: 0;
	--_responsive---xsmall: 1;
	--flex-xsmall: flex;
	--none-xsmall: none;
	--column-xsmall: column;
	--row-xsmall: row;
	--start-xsmall: start;
	--center-xsmall: center;
	--end-xsmall: end;
	--relative-xsmall: relative;
	}
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-xsmall-columns="1"] { display: flex; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-xsmall-columns="2"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 2; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-xsmall-columns="3"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 3; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-xsmall-columns="4"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 4; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-xsmall-columns="5"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 5; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-xsmall-columns="6"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 6; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-xsmall-columns="7"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 7; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-xsmall-columns="8"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 8; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-xsmall-columns="9"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 9; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-xsmall-columns="10"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 10; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-xsmall-columns="11"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 11; }
	:not([data-wf--grid--variant*="auto"]) > [data-xsmall-columns="12"] { display: grid; --_column-count---value: 12; }
	.u-order-first-xsmall { order: -1; }
	.u-order-auto-xsmall { order: 0; }
	.u-order-last-xsmall { order: 1; }
	.u-display-contents-xsmall { display: contents; }
	.u-display-block-xsmall { display: block; }
	.u-display-grid-xsmall { display: grid; }
	.u-display-flex-xsmall { display: flex; }
	.u-display-none-xsmall { display: none; }
	.u-all-unset-xsmall { all: unset; }
	.slider_offset { --slide-count: var(--xs); }
	}
	
	</style></div><div class="w-embed"><style>
	:root {
	/*--screen--2xl: 1920px;
	--screen--xl: 1512px;
	--screen--l: 1440px;
	--screen--m: 1240px;
	--screen--s: 1024px;
	--screen--xs: 767px;
	--screen--2xs: 479px;*/
	--ease: cubic-bezier(0.43, 0.195, 0.02, 1);
	}
	
	.u-transition-ease {
	transition-timing-function: var(--ease);
	}
	
	.tooltip_text_wrap::before {
	content: '';
	position: absolute;
	left: 50%;
	bottom: -0.6rem;
	width: 1.2rem;
	height: 1.2rem;
	background: var(--_theme---nav--background);
	transform: translateX(-50%) rotate(45deg);
	border-radius: 0 0 0.5rem 0; /* slight rounding on the tip */
	}
	
	@media screen and (max-width: 767px) {
	.u-l-hide {
	display: none!important;
	}
	</style></div><div class="w-embed"><style>
	:root {
	--root-font-size-base: 0.625rem;
	--root-font-scale: 0.9;
	}
	
	html {
	font-size: calc(var(--root-font-size-base) * var(--root-font-scale));
	}
	
	/* 375-1023px */
	@media (min-width: 375px) {
	:root {
	--root-font-scale: 1;
	}
	}
	
	@media (min-width: 992px) and (max-width: 1023px) {
	:root {
	--root-font-scale: 0.8;
	}
	}
	
	/* 1024-1279px */
	@media (min-width: 1024px) {
	:root {
	--root-font-scale: 0.9;
	}
	}
	
	/* 1280-1799px */
	@media (min-width: 1280px) {
	:root {
	--root-font-scale: 1;
	}
	}
	
	/* 1800-1919px */
	@media (min-width: 1800px) {
	:root {
	--root-font-scale: 1.1;
	}
	}
	
	/* 1920-2399px */
	@media (min-width: 1920px) {
	:root {
	--root-font-scale: 1.2;
	}
	}
	
	/* 2400px+ */
	@media (min-width: 2400px) {
	:root {
	--root-font-scale: 1.3;
	}
	}
	</style></div><div class="w-embed"><style>
	/* Main Button Styles */
	.button_main_text,
	.button_main_text::after {
	transition-property: transform;
	transition-duration: 0.45s;
	transition-timing-function: var(--ease);
	}
	.button_main_text::after {
	content: attr(data-label);
	position: absolute;
	left: 0;
	width: 100%;
	transform: translateY(100%);
	pointer-events: none;
	}
	html:not(.wf-design-mode) .button_main_wrap:hover .button_main_text {
	transform: translateY(-100%);
	}
	
	/* Link Button Styles */
	/** Underline Styling **/
	
	[data-link-underline-placeholder] {
	position: relative;
	}
	[data-link-underline-placeholder='true']::after {
	content: '';
	position: absolute;
	left: 0;
	bottom: 0rem;
	width: 100%;
	height: 1px;
	background-color: var(--_theme---button-link--border);
	}
	
	[data-link-underline]::before {
	content: '';
	position: absolute;
	left: 0;
	bottom: 0rem;
	width: 100%;
	height: 1px;
	background-color: var(--_theme---button-link--text);
	transform: scaleX(0);
	transform-origin: left;
	transition:
	transform 0.4s var(--ease),
	transform-origin 0s;
	}
	
	[data-underline-link-trigger]:hover [data-underline-link],
	[data-underline-link-trigger]:hover .btn_main_wrap [data-underline-link],
	[data-underline-link-trigger] .btn_main_wrap:hover [data-underline-link] {
	background-size: 100% 1px;
	background-position: 0 100%;
	}
	
	/*** Underline Links Styling (WILL BE INTEGRATED WITH GLOBAL UNDERLINE LINK INTERACTION ***/
	
	@media screen and (min-width: 992px) {
	[underline-link] {
	display: inline;
	padding-bottom: 1px;
	background-image: linear-gradient(currentColor, currentColor);
	background-repeat: no-repeat;
	background-size: 0% 1px;
	background-position: 100% 100%;
	-webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;
	box-decoration-break: clone;
	transition:
	background-size 0.4s var(--ease),
	background-position 0s;
	}
	
	[underline-link-trigger]:hover [underline-link],
	[underline-link-trigger]:hover .btn_main_wrap [underline-link],
	[underline-link-trigger] .btn_main_wrap:hover [underline-link] {
	background-size: 100% 1px;
	background-position: 0 100%;
	}
	}
	
	/* Hover in — when trigger OR button is hovered */
	[data-link-underline-trigger]:hover [data-link-underline]::before,
	[data-link-underline-trigger]:hover .button_link_wrap [data-link-underline]::before,
	.button_link_wrap:hover[data-link-underline]::before {
	transform: scaleX(1);
	transform-origin: left;
	}
	
	/* Hover out — when neither the trigger nor the button itself is hovered */
	[data-link-underline-trigger]:not(:hover) [data-link-underline]::before,
	.button_link_wrap:not(:hover)[data-link-underline]::before {
	transform: scaleX(0);
	transform-origin: right;
	}
	
	/* Icons animation (TO BE REVISED) */
	
	.btn_main_icon {
	transition: all 0.4s var(--ease);
	}
	
	/* On hover: move the icons */
	.btn_main_wrap:hover .btn_main_icon.is-first,
	[underline-link-trigger]:hover .btn_main_icon.is-first {
	right: -100%; /* Move 100% of its own width to the right */
	}
	
	.btn_main_wrap:hover .btn_main_icon.is-second,
	[underline-link-trigger]:hover .btn_main_icon.is-second {
	left: 0; /* Move to the starting position */
	}
	</style></div><div class="w-embed"><style>
	/* Themes */
	/*** White ***/
	[data-accent-theme='white'] {
	--accent-theme--bg-color: var(--swatch--white);
	--accent-theme--text-color: var(--swatch--black);
	background-color: var(--accent-theme--bg-color);
	color: var(--accent-theme--text-color);
	}
	/*** Amethyst ***/
	[data-accent-theme='amethyst'],
	[data-accent-theme='Instagram'] {
	--accent-theme--bg-color: var(--swatch--amethyst);
	--accent-theme--text-color: var(--swatch--white);
	background-color: var(--accent-theme--bg-color);
	color: var(--accent-theme--text-color);
	}
	[data-accent-theme-invert='amethyst'] {
	--accent-theme--bg-color: var(--swatch--amethyst);
	--accent-theme--text-color: var(--swatch--white);
	background-color: var(--accent-theme--text-color);
	color: var(--accent-theme--bg-color);
	}
	/*** Yellow ***/
	[data-accent-theme='hyper-yellow'] {
	--accent-theme--bg-color: var(--swatch--hyper-yellow);
	--accent-theme--text-color: var(--swatch--black);
	background-color: var(--accent-theme--bg-color) !important;
	color: var(--accent-theme--text-color) !important;

	--accent-theme--text-color: var(--swatch--black);
	background-color: var(--accent-theme--text-color);

	--accent-theme--text-color: var(--swatch--white);
	background-color: var(--accent-theme--text-color);
	color: var(--accent-theme--bg-color);
	}
	/*** Powder ***/
	[data-accent-theme='powder'] {
	--accent-theme--bg-color: var(--swatch--powder);
	--accent-theme--text-color: var(--swatch--black);
	background-color: var(--accent-theme--bg-color);
	color: var(--accent-theme--text-color);
	}
	[data-accent-theme-invert='powder'] {
	--accent-theme--bg-color: var(--swatch--powder);
	--accent-theme--text-color: var(--swatch--black);
	background-color: var(--accent-theme--text-color);
	color: var(--accent-theme--bg-color);
	}
	/*** Thistle ***/
	[data-accent-theme='thistle'] {
	--accent-theme--bg-color: var(--swatch--thistle);
	--accent-theme--text-color: var(--swatch--black);
	background-color: var(--accent-theme--bg-color);
	color: var(--accent-theme--text-color);
	}
	[data-accent-theme-invert='thistle'] {
	--accent-theme--bg-color: var(--swatch--thistle);
	--accent-theme--text-color: var(--swatch--black);
	background-color: var(--accent-theme--text-color);
	color: var(--accent-theme--bg-color);
	}
	/*** Electric Cyan ***/
	[data-accent-theme='electric-cyan'] {
	--accent-theme--bg-color: var(--swatch--electric-cyan) !important;
	--accent-theme--text-color: var(--swatch--black) !important;
	background-color: var(--accent-theme--bg-color) !important;
	color: var(--accent-theme--text-color) !important;
	}
	/*** Grey ***/
	[data-accent-theme='grey'] {
	--accent-theme--bg-color: var(--swatch--grey);
	--accent-theme--text-color: var(--swatch--black);
	background-color: var(--accent-theme--bg-color);
	color: var(--accent-theme--text-color);
	}
	[data-accent-theme-invert='grey'] {
	--accent-theme--bg-color: var(--swatch--grey);
	--accent-theme--text-color: var(--swatch--black);
	background-color: var(--accent-theme--text-color);
	color: var(--accent-theme--bg-color);
	}
	/*** White ***/
	[data-accent-theme='white'] {
	--accent-theme--bg-color: var(--swatch--white) !important;
	--accent-theme--text-color: var(--swatch--black) !important;
	background-color: var(--accent-theme--bg-color) !important;
	color: var(--accent-theme--text-color) !important;
	}
	[data-accent-theme-invert='white'] {
	--accent-theme--bg-color: var(--swatch--black);
	--accent-theme--text-color: var(--swatch--white);
	background-color: var(--accent-theme--text-color);
	color: var(--accent-theme--bg-color);
	}
	/*** Black ***/
	[data-accent-theme='black'] {
	--accent-theme--bg-color: var(--swatch--black) !important;
	--accent-theme--text-color: var(--swatch--white) !important;
	background-color: var(--accent-theme--bg-color) !important;
	color: var(--accent-theme--text-color) !important;
	}
	[data-accent-theme-invert='black'] {
	--accent-theme--bg-color: var(--swatch--white);
	--accent-theme--text-color: var(--swatch--black);
	background-color: var(--accent-theme--text-color);
	color: var(--accent-theme--bg-color);
	}
	
	/* Section Header Illustration Color */
	[data-illustration-color='vivid-orange'] {
	color: var(--swatch--vivid-orange);
	}
	[data-illustration-color='neon-pink'] {
	color: var(--swatch--neon-pink);
	}
	
	/* Footer CTA Channel-based Color */
	[data-channel='Instagram'] .footer_button .button_main_wrap {
	background: var(--swatch--amethyst);
	color: var(--swatch--white);
	}
	
	[data-channel='WhatsApp'] .footer_button .button_main_wrap {
	background: var(--swatch--hazel);
	color: var(--swatch--black);
	}
	
	[data-channel='Messenger'] .footer_button .button_main_wrap {
	background: var(--swatch--cobalt);
	color: var(--swatch--white);
	}
	
	/* Bold Text Highlights */
	.u-bold-highlight-yellow strong {
	color: var(--swatch--hyper-yellow);
	font-weight: 400;
	}
	.u-bold-highlight-pink strong {
	color: var(--swatch--neon-pink);
	font-weight: 400;
	}
	.u-bold-highlight-faded strong {
	color: color-mix(in srgb, currentColor 60%, transparent);
	font-weight: 400;
	}
	</style></div><div class="w-embed"><style>
	
	/* Column Count */
	[data-grid-pattern-columns-large="3"] { --grid-pattern-column-count: 3; }
	[data-grid-pattern-columns-large="4"] { --grid-pattern-column-count: 4; }

	[data-grid-pattern-columns-medium="3"] { --grid-pattern-column-count: 3; }
	[data-grid-pattern-columns-medium="4"] { --grid-pattern-column-count: 4; }

	[data-grid-pattern-columns-medium="12"] { --grid-pattern-column-count: 12; }
	}
	@media screen and (max-width: 35em) {

	[data-grid-pattern-columns-small="4"] { --grid-pattern-column-count: 4; }
	[data-grid-pattern-columns-small="5"] { --grid-pattern-column-count: 5; }

	[data-grid-pattern-columns-small="10"] { --grid-pattern-column-count: 10; }
	[data-grid-pattern-columns-small="11"] { --grid-pattern-column-count: 11; }
	[data-grid-pattern-columns-small="12"] { --grid-pattern-column-count: 12; }
	}
	@container (width < 20em) {
	
	}
	
	/* Grid Pattern Styling */
	.bg-grid-pattern:before {
	content: "";
	position: absolute;
	left: 0;
	top: 50%;
	transform: translateY(-50%);
	display: block;
	width: calc(100% + 2px);
	aspect-ratio: 1 / 2;
	background-image: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--_theme---border-primary) 1px, transparent 0),
	linear-gradient(180deg, var(--_theme---border-primary) 1px, transparent 0);
	background-size: calc(100% / var( --grid-pattern-column-count)) calc(100% / var(--grid-pattern-column-count) / 2);
	background-position: -1px -1px;
	}
	
	[data-accent-color-secondary] .bg-grid-pattern:before {
	background-image: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--_theme---border-secondary) 1px, transparent 0),
	linear-gradient(180deg, var(--_theme---border-secondary) 1px, transparent 0);
	}
	
	[data-accent-theme="amethyst"] .bg-grid-pattern:before,
	[data-accent-theme="cobalt"] .bg-grid-pattern:before,
	[data-accent-theme="currant"] .bg-grid-pattern:before,
	[data-accent-theme="kelp"] .bg-grid-pattern:before
	{
	background-image: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--swatch--white-o10) 1px, transparent 0),
	linear-gradient(180deg, var(--swatch--white-o10) 1px, transparent 0)!important;
	}
	
	[data-accent-theme="hyper-yellow"] .bg-grid-pattern:before,
	[data-accent-theme="ecru"] .bg-grid-pattern:before,
	[data-accent-theme="sharp-green"] .bg-grid-pattern:before,
	[data-accent-theme="hazel"] .bg-grid-pattern:before,
	[data-accent-theme="neon-pink"] .bg-grid-pattern:before,
	[data-accent-theme="thistle"] .bg-grid-pattern:before,
	[data-accent-theme="vivid-orange"] .bg-grid-pattern:before,
	[data-accent-theme="dusk"] .bg-grid-pattern:before,
	[data-accent-theme="electric-cyan"] .bg-grid-pattern:before,
	[data-accent-theme="powder"] .bg-grid-pattern:before
	{
	background-image: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--swatch--black-o10) 1px, transparent 0),
	linear-gradient(180deg, var(--swatch--black-o10) 1px, transparent 0)!important;
	}

	display: block;

	/* Nav styles (TO BE REVISED) */
	.nav_item_text_wrap {
	/*margin: 0 0.4rem;*/
	cursor: pointer;
	}
	
	/* Dropdown */
	@media (min-width: 992px) {
	.nav_dd_content {
	position: absolute;
	top: calc(100%);
	left: 0;
	width: 100%;
	height: auto;
	opacity: 0;
	pointer-events: none;
	will-change: transform;
	}
	}
	
	.dropdown_bg {
	position: absolute;
	width: 100%;
	max-width: 140rem;
	height: 0;
	border-radius: 8px;
	z-index: -1;
	}
	
	.dropdown_col {
	width: 32rem;
	padding-left: 0rem;
	padding-right: 0rem;
	}
	
	@media screen and (min-width: 769px) {
	.dropdown_col {
	border-left: 1px solid var(--_theme---border-primary);
	padding-left: 2rem;
	padding-right: 2rem;
	}
	
	.dropdown_col:first-child {
	width: 32rem;
	padding-left: 0rem;
	padding-right: 2rem;
	border-left: none;
	}
	
	.dropdown_col:last-child {
	padding-left: 2rem !important;
	padding-right: 0rem;
	border-left: 1px solid var(--_theme---border-primary);
	}
	}
	
	/* Mobile nav styling */
	.nav_burger:before,
	.nav_burger:after {
	position: absolute;
	top: 50%;
	left: 0;
	right: 0;
	margin: 0 auto;
	display: block;
	height: 1px;
	background-color: currentColor;
	content: '';
	opacity: 1;
	transition: transform 0.9s var(--ease);
	}
	
	.nav_burger:before {
	transform: translate3d(0, calc(-50% - 0.4rem), 0) rotate(0);
	}
	
	.nav_burger:after {
	transform: translate3d(0, calc(-50% + 0.4rem), 0) rotate(0);
	}
	
	.nav_burger.is-open:before {
	transform: translate3d(0, -50%, 0) rotate(225deg);
	}
	
	.nav_burger.is-open:after {
	transform: translate3d(0, -50%, 0) rotate(135deg);
	}
	
	/* Misc */
	.nav_dd_item_layout {
	cursor: pointer;
	}
	</style></div><div class="nav_dd_overlay"></div><div id="" class="nav_container u-container-full"><div class="nav_wrap"><div class="nav_blur"></div><div class="nav_left"><div class="nav_logo_wrap"><a class="locale_item_link w-inline-block"><div class="nav_dd_item_text is-language-switcher">de</div></a></div></div></div></nav></div><div class="u-embed-js w-embed w-script"><script>
	document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
	const parents = document.querySelectorAll("[data-hide-locale]");
	
	parents.forEach(parent => {
	const hideAttr = parent.getAttribute("data-hide-locale");
	if (!hideAttr) return;
	
	// Split by comma and normalize to lowercase
	const localesToHide = hideAttr
	.split(",")
	.map(locale => locale.trim().toLowerCase())
	.filter(Boolean);
	
	if (!localesToHide.length) return;
	
	// Find matching children
	const children = parent.querySelectorAll("[data-locale]");
	
	children.forEach(child => {
	const childLocale = child.getAttribute("data-locale");
	if (!childLocale) return;
	
	if (localesToHide.includes(childLocale.trim().toLowerCase())) {
	child.style.display = "none";
	}
	});
	});
	});
	</script></div></div></div><div class="nav_center"><div class="nav_center_scroll"><div class="nav_dd_bg"></div><div class="nav_menu_list"><div class="nav_dd_item"><div underline-link-trigger="" data-wf-component-context="%5B%7B%22componentId%22%3A%2270ac6d67-5ad0-6fe6-5491-4a84dfa528bd%22%2C%22instanceId%22%3A%220b591fe6-4a9b-ad7f-3331-d054202713f7%22%7D%2C%7B%22componentId%22%3A%22824b76d0-0ddb-5ed5-8ae6-09771aaa568a%22%2C%22instanceId%22%3A%2270ac6d67-5ad0-6fe6-5491-4a84dfa5291b%22%7D%2C%7B%22componentId%22%3A%220254e612-9509-0757-3332-f6be61ca6889%22%2C%22instanceId%22%3A%22e3c8b535-008d-18b4-19d8-a889182de0e1%22%7D%5D" data-wf-native-id-path="0b591fe6-4a9b-ad7f-3331-d054202713f7:70ac6d67-5ad0-6fe6-5491-4a84dfa5291b:e3c8b535-008d-18b4-19d8-a889182de0e1:0254e612-9509-0757-3332-f6be61ca688a" data-wf-ao-click-engagement-tracking="true"><span class="clickable_text u-sr-only">Sign in</span></a></div><div data-wf--button-main-element--button-size="main" class="button_main_element"><div class="button_main_text_wrap"><div data-label="Sign in" aria-hidden="true" class="button_main_text">Sign in</div></div></div></div></div><div class="nav_burger"></div></div></div></div><div class="u-embed-js w-embed w-script"><script>
	document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
	const navbar = document.querySelector(".nav_component");
	const navbarBlur = document.querySelector(".nav_blur");
	let triggerHeight = window.innerHeight * 0.1;
	
	const logoLink = document.querySelector(".nav_logo_wrap");
	const mobileBtn = document.querySelector(".nav_m_btn");
	const navbarBurger = document.querySelector(".nav_burger");
	const navbarCenter = document.querySelector(".nav_center");
	const body = document.body;
	
	if (!navbar) return;
	
	// ✅ Remember what theme the navbar had at the top initially
	const topTheme = navbar.classList.contains("u-theme-light")
	? "light"
	: "dark";
	
	let isScrolledPastTrigger = window.scrollY > triggerHeight;
	let isHoveringDropdown = false;
	let isMobileMenuOpen = false;
	
	// Logo animation
	let logoTimeline = null;
	let logoWidthTimeline = null;
	
	function animateLogo(isDown) {
	const logo = document.querySelector(".nav_logo");
	if (!logo) return;
	
	const paths = logo.querySelectorAll("path");
	if (paths.length <= 1) return;
	
	const otherPaths = Array.from(paths).slice(1);
	
	if (!logoTimeline) {
	logoTimeline = gsap.timeline({
	paused: true,
	defaults: { overwrite: "auto" },
	});
	
	logoTimeline.to([...otherPaths].reverse(), {
	y: 28,
	stagger: { each: 0.04, from: "start" },
	duration: 0.8,
	ease: "power4.inOut",
	});
	}
	
	isDown ? logoTimeline.play() : logoTimeline.reverse();
	}
	
	function getLogoTargetWidth() {
	return window.innerWidth < 768
	? "2.4rem"
	: window.innerWidth < 1200
	? "3.6rem"
	: "3.2rem";
	}
	
	function animateLogoWidth(isDown) {
	if (!logoLink) return;
	
	const targetWidth = getLogoTargetWidth();
	
	if (!logoWidthTimeline) {
	logoWidthTimeline = gsap.timeline({ paused: true });
	
	logoWidthTimeline.to(logoLink, {
	width: targetWidth,
	duration: 1,
	delay: 0.2,
	ease: "power4.inOut",
	});
	}
	
	isDown ? logoWidthTimeline.play() : logoWidthTimeline.reverse();
	}
	
	// ✅ Single source of truth: “down” = scrolled OR hovering dropdown OR mobile menu open
	function updateNavbarTheme() {
	const down =
	isScrolledPastTrigger || isHoveringDropdown || isMobileMenuOpen;
	
	// THEME
	if (down) {
	navbar.classList.remove("u-theme-dark");
	navbar.classList.add("u-theme-light");
	} else {
	if (topTheme === "light") {
	navbar.classList.add("u-theme-light");
	navbar.classList.remove("u-theme-dark");
	} else {
	navbar.classList.add("u-theme-dark");
	navbar.classList.remove("u-theme-light");
	}
	}
	
	// BLUR
	if (navbarBlur) navbarBlur.style.opacity = down ? "1" : "0";
	
	// LOGO (use `down` so it always returns properly)
	animateLogo(down);
	animateLogoWidth(down);
	
	// Mobile button remains scroll-driven (as before)
	mobileBtn?.classList.toggle("is-visible", isScrolledPastTrigger);
	}
	
	function handleScroll() {
	isScrolledPastTrigger = window.scrollY > triggerHeight;
	updateNavbarTheme();
	}
	
	window.addEventListener("scroll", handleScroll, { passive: true });
	
	// =========================
	// Dropdown logic
	// =========================
	let previousIndex = null;
	let isFirstHover = true;
	
	const items = document.querySelectorAll(".nav_dd_item");
	const triggers = document.querySelectorAll(".nav_dd_trigger");
	const bg = document.querySelector(".nav_dd_bg");
	const dropdownOverlay = document.querySelector(".nav_dd_overlay");
	const navbarCenterEl = document.querySelector(".nav_center");
	
	function getDirection(prev, current) {
	if (prev === null) return "fade";
	return current > prev ? "left" : "right";
	}
	
	function animateDropdownContent(contentEl, direction) {
	if (window.innerWidth <= 1024) return;
	
	document.querySelectorAll(".nav_dd_content").forEach((el) => {
	gsap.killTweensOf(el);
	gsap.killTweensOf(el.children);
	if (el !== contentEl) {
	gsap.set(el, { opacity: 0, x: 0, pointerEvents: "none" });
	}
	});
	
	gsap.set(contentEl, { pointerEvents: "auto" });
	
	if (direction === "fade") {
	gsap.set(contentEl, { opacity: 1, x: 0 });
	gsap.fromTo(
	contentEl.children,
	{ opacity: 0, y: 10 },
	{ opacity: 1, y: 0, stagger: 0.1, duration: 0.4, ease: "power2.out" }
	);
	} else {
	const fromVars = { opacity: 0, x: direction === "left" ? 50 : -50 };
	const toVars = { opacity: 1, x: 0, duration: 0.4, ease: "power2.out" };
	gsap.fromTo(contentEl, fromVars, toVars);
	}
	}
	
	function animateBackground(bgEl, targetHeight) {
	if (window.innerWidth <= 1024) return;
	if (!bgEl) return;
	gsap.to(bgEl, { height: targetHeight, duration: 0.3, ease: "power2.out" });
	}
	
	function showOverlay() {
	if (window.innerWidth <= 1024) return;
	if (!dropdownOverlay) return;
	gsap.to(dropdownOverlay, {
	opacity: 0.3,
	duration: 0.25,
	ease: "power2.out",
	pointerEvents: "auto",
	});
	}
	
	function hideOverlay() {
	if (window.innerWidth <= 1024) return;
	if (!dropdownOverlay) return;
	gsap.to(dropdownOverlay, {
	opacity: 0,
	duration: 0.3,
	ease: "power2.out",
	pointerEvents: "none",
	});
	}
	
	function resetDropdowns() {
	if (window.innerWidth <= 1024) return;
	
	document.querySelectorAll(".nav_dd_content").forEach((el) => {
	gsap.killTweensOf(el);
	gsap.killTweensOf(el.children);
	gsap.set(el, { opacity: 0, x: 0, pointerEvents: "none" });
	});
	
	if (bg) gsap.to(bg, { height: 0, duration: 0.3, ease: "power2.out" });
	
	previousIndex = null;
	isFirstHover = true;
	
	// ✅ Important: drop hover state BEFORE updating, so top state can restore
	isHoveringDropdown = false;
	
	hideOverlay();
	updateNavbarTheme();
	}
	
	triggers.forEach((trigger, index) => {
	trigger.addEventListener("mouseenter", () => {
	if (window.innerWidth <= 1024) return;
	
	const item = trigger.closest(".nav_dd_item");
	const content = item?.querySelector(".nav_dd_content");
	if (!content) return;
	
	const direction = isFirstHover
	? "fade"
	: getDirection(previousIndex, index);
	
	animateDropdownContent(content, direction);
	animateBackground(bg, content.offsetHeight);
	
	isHoveringDropdown = true;
	showOverlay();
	
	previousIndex = index;
	isFirstHover = false;
	
	updateNavbarTheme();
	});
	});
	
	items.forEach((item) => {
	const trigger = item.querySelector(".nav_dd_trigger");
	if (!trigger) {
	item.addEventListener("mouseenter", () => {
	if (window.innerWidth <= 1024) return;
	resetDropdowns();
	});
	}
	});
	
	navbarCenterEl?.addEventListener("mouseleave", resetDropdowns);
	
	// =========================
	// Mobile Dropdown Toggle
	// =========================
	function toggleDropdown(trigger) {
	if (window.innerWidth > 1024) return;
	
	const content = trigger.nextElementSibling;
	if (!content) return;
	
	if (content.style.height && content.classList.contains("open")) {
	content.style.height = content.scrollHeight + "px";
	requestAnimationFrame(() => {
	content.style.height = "0";
	});
	content.classList.remove("open");
	} else {
	content.style.height = content.scrollHeight + "px";
	content.classList.add("open");
	
	content.addEventListener("transitionend", function handleEnd() {
	if (content.classList.contains("open")) {
	content.style.height = "auto";
	}
	content.removeEventListener("transitionend", handleEnd);
	});
	}
	}
	
	triggers.forEach((trigger) => {
	trigger.addEventListener("click", () => toggleDropdown(trigger));
	});
	
	// =========================
	// Resize: react only to WIDTH changes + refresh collapsed width
	// =========================
	let lastWidth = window.innerWidth;
	
	window.addEventListener("resize", () => {
	const currentWidth = window.innerWidth;
	
	// Ignore height-only resizes (iOS Safari address bar)
	if (currentWidth === lastWidth) return;
	
	lastWidth = currentWidth;
	
	triggerHeight = window.innerHeight * 0.1;
	
	// Rebuild width timeline so it uses new breakpoint targetWidth
	if (logoWidthTimeline) {
	logoWidthTimeline.kill();
	logoWidthTimeline = null;
	}
	
	if (window.innerWidth > 1024) {
	document.querySelectorAll(".nav_dd_content").forEach((content) => {
	content.style.height = "";
	content.classList.remove("open");
	});
	}
	
	isScrolledPastTrigger = window.scrollY > triggerHeight;
	updateNavbarTheme();
	});
	
	// =========================
	// Mobile Menu Toggle & Scroll Lock
	// =========================
	if (navbarBurger && navbarCenter) {
	navbarBurger.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
	e.preventDefault?.();
	
	isMobileMenuOpen = navbarCenter.classList.toggle("is-open");
	navbarBurger.classList.toggle("is-open");
	
	body.style.overflow = isMobileMenuOpen ? "hidden" : "";
	
	// Keep mobileBtn hidden when menu opens (your original behavior)
	if (isMobileMenuOpen) mobileBtn?.classList.remove("is-visible");
	
	updateNavbarTheme();
	});
	}
	
	// Initial paint
	updateNavbarTheme();
	});
	</script></div></div><section class="hero_section u-min-height-screen u-position-relative u-theme-dark"><div class="u-embed-css w-embed"><style>

	document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
	document.querySelectorAll("[data-badge-code-copy]").forEach((trigger) => {
	const badge = trigger.querySelector("[data-badge-code]");
	if (!badge) return;
	
	const originalValue =
	badge.getAttribute("data-badge-code")?.trim() ||
	badge.textContent.trim();
	
	let timeoutId;
	
	trigger.addEventListener("click", async () => {
	const value =
	badge.getAttribute("data-badge-code")?.trim() ||
	originalValue;
	
	if (!value || value === "copied!") return;
	
	try {
	await navigator.clipboard.writeText(value);
	
	badge.setAttribute("data-badge-code", "copied!");
	badge.textContent = "copied!";
	
	clearTimeout(timeoutId);
	timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
	badge.setAttribute("data-badge-code", originalValue);
	badge.textContent = originalValue;
	}, 2000);
	} catch (error) {
	console.error("Copy failed:", error);
	}
	});
	});
	});
	</script></div></div></div></div></div><div class="sp_badges"><div class="sp_badges_component"><div class="sp_badges_group"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 148 32" w-embed"><style>
	html:not(.wf-design-mode) .hero_ui_wrap [data-ui-card] {
	opacity: 0;
	transform: translate3d(0, 0, 0);
	}
	</style></div><div class="hero_ui_wrap"><img data-ui-card="" loading="lazy" alt="" src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/68e8cfb1ceca15023589ca9f/6977253a3923a91a495a981e_home-hero-bubble_01v2.png" sizes="100vw" srcset="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/68e8cfb1ceca15023589ca9f/6977253a3923a91a495a981e_home-hero-bubble_01v2-p-500.png 500w, https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/68e8cfb1ceca15023589ca9f/6977253a3923a91a495a981e_home-hero-bubble_01v2.png 720w" class="hero_ui_card"/><img data-ui-card="" loading="lazy" alt="" src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/68e8cfb1ceca15023589ca9f/6977253a3923a91a495a9816_home-hero-bubble_02v2.png" sizes="100vw" srcset="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/68e8cfb1ceca15023589ca9f/6977253a3923a91a495a9816_home-hero-bubble_02v2-p-500.png 500w, https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/68e8cfb1ceca15023589ca9f/6977253a3923a91a495a9816_home-hero-bubble_02v2.png 720w" class="hero_ui_card"/><img data-ui-card="" loading="lazy" alt="" src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/68e8cfb1ceca15023589ca9f/6977253a3923a91a495a981a_home-hero-bubble_03v2.png" sizes="100vw" srcset="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/68e8cfb1ceca15023589ca9f/6977253a3923a91a495a981a_home-hero-bubble_03v2-p-500.png 500w, https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/68e8cfb1ceca15023589ca9f/6977253a3923a91a495a981a_home-hero-bubble_03v2.png 720w" class="hero_ui_card"/></div><div class="u-embed-js w-embed w-script"><script>
	document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
	document.querySelectorAll(".hero_ui_wrap").forEach(initUICards);
	
	function initUICards(wrap) {
	// guard against double init (Webflow often runs embeds multiple times)
	if (wrap.dataset.uiCardsInit === "true") return;
	wrap.dataset.uiCardsInit = "true";
	
	const cards = Array.from(wrap.querySelectorAll("[data-ui-card]"));
	if (cards.length < 2) return;
	
	const cfg = {
	// durations
	appear: 0.65, // fade in duration
	shift: 0.65, // move/stack duration
	holdSolo: 1, // hold when only first card is visible
	holdPair: 1, // hold when pair is visible
	exit: 0.5, // final exit duration
	staggerExit: 0.2, // stagger on last two
	noneGap: 0.3, // "no visible cards" pause before looping
	
	ease: "power2.out",
	
	// layout / motion
	gap: 12, // px space between stacked cards (set 0 if none)
	startY: 40, // px offset for "coming in" (from below)
	exitY: -30, // px additional move on exit (upwards)
	};
	
	if (getComputedStyle(wrap).position === "static") {
	wrap.style.position = "relative";
	}
	
	// Positioning: stack cards in the same spot (respect your bottom:4rem setup)
	cards.forEach((c, idx) => {
	c.style.position = "absolute";
	c.style.left = "auto";
	c.style.right = "auto";
	c.style.top = "auto";
	c.style.bottom = "4rem";
	//c.style.willChange = "transform, opacity";
	c.style.zIndex = idx + 1;
	});
	
	// timeline paused until built
	const tl = gsap.timeline({
	repeat: -1,
	paused: true,
	defaults: { ease: cfg.ease },
	});
	
	let heights = [];
	
	function measure() {
	heights = cards.map((el) => el.getBoundingClientRect().height);
	const maxH = Math.max(...heights);
	if (maxH) wrap.style.minHeight = maxH + "px";
	}
	
	// quick helpers
	const hideAll = (pos) => tl.set(cards, { opacity: 0 }, pos);
	const showOnly = (idxs, pos) => {
	hideAll(pos);
	idxs.forEach((i) => tl.set(cards[i], { opacity: 1 }, pos));
	};
	
	function hardResetNow() {
	gsap.killTweensOf(cards);
	gsap.set(cards, { clearProps: "opacity,transform" }); // remove inline leftovers
	gsap.set(cards, { opacity: 0, y: 0, overwrite: true });
	}
	
	function build() {
	tl.pause(0).clear().time(0);
	
	hardResetNow();
	measure();
	
	// timeline cursor (THIS prevents "everything at once")
	let t = 0;
	
	// ===== STEP 1: first card appears alone =====
	showOnly([0], t);
	tl.set(cards[0], { opacity: 0, y: cfg.startY }, t);
	tl.to(
	cards[0],
	{ opacity: 1, y: 0, duration: cfg.appear, immediateRender: false },
	t
	);
	t += cfg.appear + cfg.holdSolo;
	
	// From here on, we maintain:
	// at end of each step i: only (i-1) at y = -shift and i at y=0 are visible
	// and if i>=2, card (i-2) is faded out during the step.
	
	for (let i = 1; i < cards.length; i++) {
	const prev = i - 1; // will move up
	const curr = i; // appears and becomes the "base"
	const old = i - 2; // fades out (when exists)
	
	// The new shift distance is based on the *new card's height*
	const shiftBy = (heights[curr] || 0) + cfg.gap;
	
	// Only prev & curr visible during this step
	showOnly([prev, curr], t);
	
	// Ensure curr starts hidden below, then fades in
	tl.set(cards[curr], { opacity: 0, y: cfg.startY }, t);
	tl.to(
	cards[curr],
	{ opacity: 1, duration: cfg.appear, immediateRender: false },
	t
	);
	
	// Move prev up by shiftBy; move curr into place at y=0
	// (curr y goes from startY -> 0 during the shift so it "settles")
	tl.to(cards[prev], { y: `-=${shiftBy}`, duration: cfg.shift }, t);
	tl.to(cards[curr], { y: 0, duration: cfg.shift }, t);
	
	// Fade out + move up the "old" card (if it exists) during same shift
	if (old >= 0) {
	// old is currently at y = -previousShift; push it further up by shiftBy and fade it
	// Make sure it doesn't suddenly become visible:
	tl.set(cards[old], { opacity: 1 }, t); // it was visible in the previous step
	tl.to(
	cards[old],
	{ opacity: 0, y: `-=${shiftBy}`, duration: cfg.shift },
	t
	);
	}
	
	t += cfg.shift + cfg.holdPair;
	}
	
	// ===== FINAL: last two exit with slight stagger =====
	const a = cards.length - 2;
	const b = cards.length - 1;
	
	showOnly([a, b], t);
	
	tl.to(
	cards[a],
	{ opacity: 0, y: `+=${cfg.exitY}`, duration: cfg.exit },
	t
	);
	tl.to(
	cards[b],
	{ opacity: 0, y: `+=${cfg.exitY}`, duration: cfg.exit },
	t + cfg.staggerExit
	);
	
	t += cfg.exit + cfg.staggerExit;
	
	// ===== NONE visible gap then reset =====
	hideAll(t);
	t += cfg.noneGap;
	
	tl.set(cards, { opacity: 0, y: 0 }, t);
	
	tl.play(0);
	}
	
	// Wait for images to load so heights are correct
	const imgs = cards.filter((el) => el.tagName === "IMG");
	Promise.all(
	imgs.map((img) =>
	img.complete
	? Promise.resolve()
	: new Promise((res) =>
	img.addEventListener("load", res, { once: true })
	)
	)
	).then(build);
	
	// ─── Resize: only rebuild when WIDTH changes ───────────────────────────
	// On mobile, browser chrome (address bar) showing/hiding fires a resize
	// event with a height-only change. Guarding on width prevents the
	// animation from restarting every time the user scrolls.
	let lastWidth = window.innerWidth;
	let rAF = null;
	
	window.addEventListener("resize", () => {
	const newWidth = window.innerWidth;
	if (newWidth === lastWidth) return; // height-only change — ignore
	lastWidth = newWidth;
	
	cancelAnimationFrame(rAF);
	rAF = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
	hardResetNow();
	build();
	});
	});
	}
	});
	</script></div></div><div data-wf--spacer--section-space="lg-180px" id="" class="u-section-spacer w-variant-60a7ad7d-02b0-6682-95a5-2218e6fd1490 u-ignore-trim"></div><div class="hero_arrow_wrap u-l-hide"><div class="u-embed-css w-embed"><style>
	
	.hero_arrow_wrap {
	animation: arrowFloat 2.5s var(--ease) infinite;
	}
	
	@keyframes arrowFloat {
	0% {
	transform: translateY(-40%);
	opacity: 0;
	}
	20% {
	transform: translateY(0%);
	opacity: 1;
	}
	60% {
	transform: translateY(40%);
	opacity: 0;
	}
	100% {
	transform: translateY(40%);
	opacity: 0; /* small delay before restarting */
	}
	}
	
	</style></div><div data-wf--icon-wrapper--icon-size="m" STYLE="" class="icon_wrap"><div class="icon_slot w-variant-1fe28dc7-4544-d8fd-a603-997c63ce6c49"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none" class="icon"><path d="M17 11H14.9296L10 15.9296L5.0704 11H3L10 18L17 11Z" fill="currentColor"></path><path d="M10.8 11L10.8 2L9.3 2L9.3 11H10.8Z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg></div></div></div></section><main id="main" class="page_main"><section><div class="testimonials_wrap"><div class="u-embed-css w-embed"><style>

	@container (width < 35em) {

	@container (width < 20em) {

	}
	
	.slider_list > *,
	.slider_list > .w-dyn-list > * > * > *,
	.slider_list > .u-display-contents > .w-dyn-list > * > * > *,

	<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/swiper@8/swiper-bundle.min.js" defer></script>
	<script>
	document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
	document.querySelectorAll("[data-slider='component']:not([data-slider='component'] [data-slider='component'])").forEach((component) => {
	if (component.dataset.scriptInitialized) return;
	component.dataset.scriptInitialized = 'true';
	
	const swiperElement = component.querySelector('.slider_element');
	const swiperWrapper = component.querySelector('.slider_list');
	if (!swiperElement || !swiperWrapper) return;
	
	function flattenDisplayContents(slot) {
	if (!slot) return;
	let child = slot.firstElementChild;
	while (child && child.classList.contains('u-display-contents')) {
	while (child.firstChild) {
	slot.insertBefore(child.firstChild, child);
	}
	slot.removeChild(child);
	child = slot.firstElementChild;
	}
	}
	flattenDisplayContents(swiperWrapper);
	
	function removeCMSList(slot) {
	const dynList = Array.from(slot.children).find((child) => child.classList.contains('w-dyn-list'));
	if (!dynList) return;
	const nestedItems = dynList?.firstElementChild?.children;
	if (!nestedItems) return;
	const staticWrapper = [...slot.children];
	[...nestedItems].forEach((el) => {
	const c = [...el.children].find((c) => !c.classList.contains('w-condition-invisible'));
	c && slot.appendChild(c);
	});
	staticWrapper.forEach((el) => el.remove());
	}
	removeCMSList(swiperWrapper);
	
	[...swiperWrapper.children].forEach((el) => el.classList.add('swiper-slide'));
	
	const followFinger = swiperElement.getAttribute('data-follow-finger') === 'true',
	freeMode = swiperElement.getAttribute('data-free-mode') === 'true',
	mousewheel = swiperElement.getAttribute('data-mousewheel') === 'true',
	slideToClickedSlide = swiperElement.getAttribute('data-slide-to-clicked') === 'true',
	loop = swiperElement.getAttribute('data-loop') === 'true',
	centeredSlides = swiperElement.getAttribute('data-centered-slides') === 'true',
	initialSlide = swiperElement.hasAttribute('data-initial-slide') ? +swiperElement.getAttribute('data-initial-slide') : 0,
	grabCursor = swiperElement.getAttribute('data-grab-cursor') === 'true',
	touchRatio = swiperElement.hasAttribute('data-touch-ratio') ? +swiperElement.getAttribute('data-touch-ratio') : 1,
	threshold = swiperElement.hasAttribute('data-threshold') ? +swiperElement.getAttribute('data-threshold') : 8,
	speed = +swiperElement.getAttribute('data-speed') || 600;
	
	new Swiper(swiperElement, {
	slidesPerView: 'auto',
	followFinger: followFinger,
	loop: loop,
	loopAdditionalSlides: 10,
	freeMode: freeMode,
	slideToClickedSlide: slideToClickedSlide,
	centeredSlides: centeredSlides,
	initialSlide: initialSlide,
	grabCursor: grabCursor,
	touchRatio: touchRatio,
	threshold: threshold,
	touchStartPreventDefault: false,
	preventClicks: true,
	preventClicksPropagation: true,
	autoHeight: false,
	speed: speed,
	mousewheel: {
	enabled: mousewheel,
	forceToAxis: true,
	},
	keyboard: {
	enabled: true,
	onlyInViewport: true,
	},
	navigation: {
	nextEl: component.querySelector("[data-slider='next'] button"),
	prevEl: component.querySelector("[data-slider='previous'] button"),
	},
	pagination: {
	el: component.querySelector('.slider_bullet_list'),
	bulletActiveClass: 'is-active',
	bulletClass: 'slider_bullet_item',
	bulletElement: 'button',
	clickable: true,
	},
	slideActiveClass: 'is-active',
	slideDuplicateActiveClass: 'is-active',
	});
	});
	});
	</script></div></div></div></div></div></div></section><section data-wf--section-features--global-theme="light" class="features_section"><div data-wf--spacer--section-space="lg-180px" id="" class="u-section-spacer w-variant-60a7ad7d-02b0-6682-95a5-2218e6fd1490 u-ignore-trim"></div><div class="section_contain u-container"><div class="section_header_component"><div 	document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
	document.querySelectorAll("[data-badge-code-copy]").forEach((trigger) => {
	const badge = trigger.querySelector("[data-badge-code]");
	if (!badge) return;
	
	const originalValue =
	badge.getAttribute("data-badge-code")?.trim() ||
	badge.textContent.trim();
	
	let timeoutId;
	
	trigger.addEventListener("click", async () => {
	const value =
	badge.getAttribute("data-badge-code")?.trim() ||
	originalValue;
	
	if (!value || value === "copied!") return;
	
	try {
	await navigator.clipboard.writeText(value);
	
	badge.setAttribute("data-badge-code", "copied!");
	badge.textContent = "copied!";
	
	clearTimeout(timeoutId);
	timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
	badge.setAttribute("data-badge-code", originalValue);
	badge.textContent = originalValue;
	}, 2000);
	} catch (error) {
	console.error("Copy failed:", error);
	}
	});
	});
	});
	</script></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="u-embed-js w-embed w-script"><script>
	document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
	const featuresContentItems = document.querySelectorAll('.features_item_component');
	const bulletsContainer = document.querySelector('.features_bullets');
	
	// Generate bullets based on content items
	const existingBullets = bulletsContainer.querySelectorAll('.bullet').length;
	const totalBulletsNeeded = featuresContentItems.length;
	
	for (let i = existingBullets; i < totalBulletsNeeded; i++) {
	const bullet = document.createElement('div');
	bullet.classList.add('bullet');
	bulletsContainer.appendChild(bullet);
	}
	
	const bullets = bulletsContainer.querySelectorAll('.bullet');
	
	featuresContentItems.forEach((item, index) => {
	ScrollTrigger.create({
	trigger: item,
	start: 'top center+=25%', // Adjusts to approximately 50% from the top
	end: 'bottom center+=25%',
	onEnter: () => activateBullet(index),
	onEnterBack: () => activateBullet(index),
	});
	});
	
	function activateBullet(index) {
	bullets.forEach((bullet) => bullet.classList.remove('is-active'));
	if (bullets[index]) {
	bullets[index].classList.add('is-active');
	}
	}
	});
	</script>
	
	<script>
	document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
	gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);
	
	const trigger = document.querySelector('[data-bullets-animation="trigger"]');
	const targets = gsap.utils.toArray('[data-bullets-animation="trigger"] .bullet');
	
	if (!trigger || !targets.length) return;
	
	gsap.from(targets, {
	yPercent: 200,
	opacity: 0,
	duration: 0.5,
	ease: 'power3.out',
	stagger: 0.2,
	scrollTrigger: {
	trigger: trigger,
	start: 'top 30%',
	toggleActions: 'play none none reverse',
	},
	});
	});
	</script></div></section><div data-wf--spacer--section-space="none" id="" class="u-section-spacer u-ignore-trim"></div></section><section STYLE="" data-accent-theme="hyper-yellow" class="channels_section"><div data-wf--spacer--section-space="lg-180px" id="" class="u-section-spacer w-variant-60a7ad7d-02b0-6682-95a5-2218e6fd1490 u-ignore-trim"></div><div class="section_contain u-container-small"><div data-wf--section-header--variant="base" class="section_header_component"><div data-wf--spacer--section-space="none" id="" class="u-section-spacer u-ignore-trim"></div><div class="section_header_group"><div data-reveal-animation="scroll"></div><h2 data-reveal-animation="scroll" data-number="13" data-wf--section-heading--size="5xl" class="section_header_title u-text-style-6xl u-margin-bottom-text">Everywhere your audience is</h2></div><div data-wf--spacer--section-space="xs-48px" id="" class="u-section-spacer w-variant-d422cbd0-f212-c815-68df-63414354c21d u-ignore-trim"></div></div><div data-slider="component" class="slider_wrap u-gap-gutter"><div class="u-embed-css w-embed"><style>
	.slider_offset {
	--slide-count: var(--lg);
	--lg: 3;
	--md: var(--lg);
	--sm: var(--md);
	--xs: var(--sm);
	}
	@container (width < 50em) {
	.slider_offset { --slide-count: var(--md); }
	}
	@container (width < 35em) {
	.slider_offset { --slide-count: var(--sm); }
	}
	@container (width < 20em) {
	.slider_offset { --slide-count: var(--xs); }
	}
	
	.slider_list > *,
	.slider_list > .w-dyn-list > * > * > *,
	.slider_list > .u-display-contents > .w-dyn-list > * > * > *,
	.slider_list > .u-display-contents > .u-display-contents > .w-dyn-list > * > * > * {
	padding-inline: calc(var(--_gap---size) / 2);
	height: auto !important;
	flex: 0 0 auto;
	width: calc(100% / var(--slide-count)) !important;
	}
	
	.swiper-wrapper {
	transition-timing-function: var(--ease) !important;
	}
	
	.wf-design-mode .slider_list.swiper-wrapper {
	overflow: scroll;
	}
	
	.swiper-slide {
	transition: opacity 0.4s var(--ease), transform 0.4s var(--ease);

href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/swiper@8/swiper-bundle.min.css"/>
	<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/swiper@8/swiper-bundle.min.js" defer></script>
	
	<script>
	document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
	const BREAKPOINT = 991;
	
	document
	.querySelectorAll("[data-slider='component']:not([data-slider='component'] [data-slider='component'])")
	.forEach((component) => {
	if (component.dataset.scriptInitialized) return;
	component.dataset.scriptInitialized = "true";
	
	const swiperElement = component.querySelector(".slider_element");
	const swiperWrapper = component.querySelector(".slider_list");
	if (!swiperElement || !swiperWrapper) return;
	
	let swiperInstance = null;
	
	/* ---------------- Helpers ---------------- */
	
	function flattenDisplayContents(slot) {
	if (!slot) return;
	let child = slot.firstElementChild;
	while (child && child.classList.contains("u-display-contents")) {
	while (child.firstChild) {
	slot.insertBefore(child.firstChild, child);
	}
	slot.removeChild(child);
	child = slot.firstElementChild;
	}
	}
	
	function removeCMSList(slot) {
	const dynList = Array.from(slot.children).find((c) =>
	c.classList.contains("w-dyn-list")
	);
	if (!dynList) return;
	
	const nestedItems = dynList?.firstElementChild?.children;
	if (!nestedItems) return;
	
	const staticWrapper = [...slot.children];
	[...nestedItems].forEach((el) => {
	const content = [...el.children].find(
	(c) => !c.classList.contains("w-condition-invisible")
	);
	if (content) slot.appendChild(content);
	});
	staticWrapper.forEach((el) => el.remove());
	}
	
	flattenDisplayContents(swiperWrapper);
	removeCMSList(swiperWrapper);
	
	[...swiperWrapper.children].forEach((el) =>
	el.classList.add("swiper-slide")
	);
	
	/* ---------------- Swiper Init / Destroy ---------------- */
	
	function initSwiper() {
	if (swiperInstance || window.innerWidth <= BREAKPOINT) return;
	
	const followFinger = swiperElement.getAttribute("data-follow-finger") === "true";
	const freeMode = swiperElement.getAttribute("data-free-mode") === "true";
	const mousewheel = swiperElement.getAttribute("data-mousewheel") === "true";
	const slideToClickedSlide =
	swiperElement.getAttribute("data-slide-to-clicked") === "true";
	const loop = swiperElement.getAttribute("data-loop") === "true";
	const centeredSlides =
	swiperElement.getAttribute("data-centered-slides") === "true";
	const speed = +swiperElement.getAttribute("data-speed") || 600;
	
	swiperInstance = new Swiper(swiperElement, {
	slidesPerView: "auto",
	followFinger,
	loop,
	loopAdditionalSlides: 10,
	freeMode,
	slideToClickedSlide,
	centeredSlides,
	autoHeight: false,
	speed,
	mousewheel: {
	enabled: mousewheel,
	forceToAxis: true,
	},
	keyboard: {
	enabled: true,
	onlyInViewport: true,
	},
	navigation: {
	nextEl: component.querySelector("[data-slider='next'] button"),
	prevEl: component.querySelector("[data-slider='previous'] button"),
	},
	pagination: {
	el: component.querySelector(".slider_bullet_list"),
	bulletActiveClass: "is-active",
	bulletClass: "slider_bullet_item",
	bulletElement: "button",
	clickable: true,
	},
	slideActiveClass: "is-active",
	slideDuplicateActiveClass: "is-active",
	});
	}
	
	function destroySwiper() {
	if (!swiperInstance || window.innerWidth > BREAKPOINT) return;
	swiperInstance.destroy(true, true);
	swiperInstance = null;
	}
	
	/* ---------------- Initial + Resize ---------------- */
	
	initSwiper();
	destroySwiper();
	
	window.addEventListener("resize", () => {
	initSwiper();
	destroySwiper();
	});
	});
	});
	</script></div></div></div><div data-wf--spacer--section-space="lg-180px" id="" class="u-section-spacer w-variant-60a7ad7d-02b0-6682-95a5-2218e6fd1490 u-ignore-trim"></div></section><section STYLE="" 	document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
	document.querySelectorAll("[data-badge-code-copy]").forEach((trigger) => {
	const badge = trigger.querySelector("[data-badge-code]");
	if (!badge) return;
	
	const originalValue =
	badge.getAttribute("data-badge-code")?.trim() ||
	badge.textContent.trim();
	
	let timeoutId;
	
	trigger.addEventListener("click", async () => {
	const value =
	badge.getAttribute("data-badge-code")?.trim() ||
	originalValue;
	
	if (!value || value === "copied!") return;
	
	try {
	await navigator.clipboard.writeText(value);
	
	badge.setAttribute("data-badge-code", "copied!");
	badge.textContent = "copied!";
	
	clearTimeout(timeoutId);
	timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
	badge.setAttribute("data-badge-code", originalValue);
	badge.textContent = originalValue;
	}, 2000);
	} catch (error) {
	console.error("Copy failed:", error);
	}
	});
	});
	});
	</script></div></div></div></div><div data-wf--before-after-card--variant="after" id="" class="ba_card_wrap w-variant-9e4c12af-20f7-6480-bc27-27d31398a04a"><div id="" class="ba_card_group 	document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
	document.querySelectorAll("[data-badge-code-copy]").forEach((trigger) => {
	const badge = trigger.querySelector("[data-badge-code]");
	if (!badge) return;
	
	const originalValue =
	badge.getAttribute("data-badge-code")?.trim() ||
	badge.textContent.trim();
	
	let timeoutId;
	
	trigger.addEventListener("click", async () => {
	const value =
	badge.getAttribute("data-badge-code")?.trim() ||
	originalValue;
	
	if (!value || value === "copied!") return;
	
	try {
	await navigator.clipboard.writeText(value);
	
	badge.setAttribute("data-badge-code", "copied!");
	badge.textContent = "copied!";
	
	clearTimeout(timeoutId);
	timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
	badge.setAttribute("data-badge-code", originalValue);
	badge.textContent = originalValue;
	}, 2000);
	} catch (error) {
	console.error("Copy failed:", error);
	}
	});
	});
	});
	</script></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="u-embed-js w-embed w-script"><script>
	document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
	gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);
	
	// 1) Force the initial state (and make sure there's a dasharray)
	gsap.set(".ba_card_illustration path", {
	strokeDasharray: 1500,
	strokeDashoffset: 1500
	});
	
	// 2) Scroll-linked draw
	gsap.to(".ba_card_illustration path", {
	strokeDashoffset: 0,
	ease: "none",
	scrollTrigger: {
	trigger: ".ba_wrap",
	start: "top top",
	end: "bottom 100%",
	scrub: true,
	immediateRender: false // prevent any jump to end on init
	// markers: true
	}
	});
	});
	
	// 3) If layout shifts after load (images/fonts), recalc positions
	window.addEventListener("load", () => {
	if (window.ScrollTrigger) {
	ScrollTrigger.refresh();
	}
	});
	</script></div></div></div></section><section><div data-wf--spacer--section-space="lg-180px" id="" class="u-section-spacer w-variant-60a7ad7d-02b0-6682-95a5-2218e6fd1490 u-ignore-trim"></div><div class="section_contain u-container"><div class="section_header_component"><div class="section_header_group"><div data-illustration-color="vivid-orange" class="section_header_icon_slot"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 80 80" fill="none" class="icon"><path d="M28.1612 38.3622L26.5714 16.6465L42.3483 29.5849L61.2725 16.8578L51.9199 37.1756L68.1916 60.6143L41.4885 57.0709L32.3305 71.0008L30.4162 52.5522L13.8281 48.5293L28.1612 38.3622Z" fill="currentColor"></path><path d="M16.0015 33.7781L6 30.0884L9.81242 22.4082L19.165 29.3894L16.0015 33.7781Z" fill="currentColor"></path><path d="M19.4441 53.2578L12.1924 63.3517L17.1242 67.781L24.6111 55.4847L19.4441 53.2578Z" fill="currentColor"></path><path d="M45.5022 13.5003L50.8883 3L43.2715 3.30883L40.0918 11.6717L45.5022 13.5003Z" fill="currentColor"></path><path d="M63.8855 34.2885L72.5973 33.8984L74.0006 41.7655L62.6201 39.3843L63.8855 34.2885Z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg></div><h2 data-number="18" class="section_header_title u-text-style-5xl u-margin-bottom-text u-text-wrap-default">From simple welcome messages to optimized sales &amp; marketing funnels</h2><div data-reveal-animation="" data-number="45" data-wf--paragraph--variant="base-h6-body" class="u-text w-richtext u-margin-bottom-0 u-text-wrap-dafault"><p>Launch fast with Quick Automations or build custom flows.</p></div></div><div data-wf--spacer--section-space="md-100px" id="" class="u-section-spacer w-variant-8cc18b30-4618-8767-0111-f6abfe45aaa3 u-ignore-trim"></div></div><div data-reveal-animation="scroll" class="hiw_component"><div class="hiw_component"><div class="u-embed-css w-embed"><style>

	document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
	document.querySelectorAll("[data-badge-code-copy]").forEach((trigger) => {
	const badge = trigger.querySelector("[data-badge-code]");
	if (!badge) return;
	
	const originalValue =
	badge.getAttribute("data-badge-code")?.trim() ||
	badge.textContent.trim();
	
	let timeoutId;
	
	trigger.addEventListener("click", async () => {
	const value =
	badge.getAttribute("data-badge-code")?.trim() ||
	originalValue;
	
	if (!value || value === "copied!") return;
	
	try {
	await navigator.clipboard.writeText(value);
	
	badge.setAttribute("data-badge-code", "copied!");
	badge.textContent = "copied!";
	
	clearTimeout(timeoutId);
	timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
	badge.setAttribute("data-badge-code", originalValue);
	badge.textContent = originalValue;
	}, 2000);
	} catch (error) {
	console.error("Copy failed:", error);
	}
	});
	});
	});
	</script></div></div></div></div></div><div class="u-embed-js w-embed w-script"><script>
	const hiwTabs = document.querySelectorAll(".hiw_tab_item");
	const contents = document.querySelectorAll(".hiw_content_item");
	
	// âœ… Fix: Use hiwTabs here instead of undefined "tabs"
	let activeIndex = document.querySelector(".hiw_tab_item.is-active")
	? Array.from(hiwTabs).indexOf(
	document.querySelector(".hiw_tab_item.is-active")
	)
	: 0;
	
	// âœ… Ensure the first tab is marked active if none is set
	if (!document.querySelector(".hiw_tab_item.is-active")) {
	hiwTabs[0].classList.add("is-active");
	}
	
	// Set initial content visible
	contents.forEach((content, index) => {
	if (index === activeIndex) {
	content.style.opacity = "1";
	content.style.transform = "translateY(0)";
	content.style.zIndex = "1";
	} else {
	content.style.opacity = "0";
	content.style.transform = "translateY(100px)";
	content.style.zIndex = "0";
	}
	});
	
	hiwTabs.forEach((tab, tabIndex) => {
	tab.addEventListener("click", () => {
	if (tabIndex === activeIndex) return; // Do nothing if clicking the already active tab
	
	// Remove active class from all tabs
	hiwTabs.forEach((t) => t.classList.remove("is-active"));
	tab.classList.add("is-active");
	
	const currentContent = contents[activeIndex];
	const nextContent = contents[tabIndex];
	
	// Animate current content out
	currentContent.style.transition = "transform 0.5s ease, opacity 0.5s ease";
	currentContent.style.transform = "translateY(-50px)";
	currentContent.style.opacity = "0";
	currentContent.style.zIndex = "0";
	
	// Prepare next content
	nextContent.style.transition = "none";
	nextContent.style.transform = "translateY(100px)";
	nextContent.style.opacity = "0";
	nextContent.style.zIndex = "1";
	
	// Animate next content in
	requestAnimationFrame(() => {
	requestAnimationFrame(() => {
	nextContent.style.transition = "transform 0.5s ease, opacity 0.5s ease";
	nextContent.style.transform = "translateY(0)";
	nextContent.style.opacity = "1";
	});
	});
	
	activeIndex = tabIndex;
	});
	});
	</script></div></div></div></div><div data-wf--spacer--section-space="none" id="" class="u-section-spacer u-ignore-trim"></div></section><section STYLE="" class="process_section"><div In fact, we&#x27;re one of the very few companies that works as a partner with TikTok and an Official Meta Business Partner.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div role="listitem" class="accordion_item"><div data-accordion="component" class="accordion_component"><h3 data-accordion="toggle-button" class="accordion_toggle_heading"><button aria-expanded="false" class="accordion_toggle_button"><span class="accordion_toggle_text u-text-style-h5 u-transition-ease">What are the main uses of Chatcoach?</span><span class="accordion_toggle_icon u-transition-ease"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" class="icon"><path d="M15.9174 9L12.0496 12.9344L8.08264 9L6 9L12.0496 15L18 9L15.9174 9Z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg></span></button></h3><div data-accordion="content-wrap" class="accordion_content_wrap"><div class="accordion_content_padding"><div class="accordion_content_text u-rich-text u-color-faded w-richtext"><p>Creators, small businesses and brands all over the world use us to send links, collect emails, grow followers, boost engagement, attract leads, drive sales, and respond to customers. You can streamline your entire operation while answering FAQs and converting followers immediately 24/7/365.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div role="listitem" class="accordion_item"><div data-accordion="component" class="accordion_component"><h3 data-accordion="toggle-button" class="accordion_toggle_heading"><button aria-expanded="false" class="accordion_toggle_button"><span class="accordion_toggle_text u-text-style-h5 u-transition-ease">Will using automations make conversations sound like a robot, or can it mimic my voice?</span><span class="accordion_toggle_icon u-transition-ease"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" class="icon"><path d="M15.9174 9L12.0496 12.9344L8.08264 9L6 9L12.0496 15L18 9L15.9174 9Z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg></span></button></h3><div data-accordion="content-wrap" class="accordion_content_wrap"><div class="accordion_content_padding"><div class="accordion_content_text u-rich-text u-color-faded w-richtext"><p>*Beep* *boop* 🤖 Just kidding. It’s your automation — you can make it say (almost) anything you want. Use slang, tell dad jokes, speak pig latin — whatever your customers expect.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div role="listitem" class="accordion_item"><div data-accordion="component" class="accordion_component"><h3 data-accordion="toggle-button" class="accordion_toggle_heading"><button aria-expanded="false" class="accordion_toggle_button"><span class="accordion_toggle_text u-text-style-h5 u-transition-ease">Do I need to know how to code to set up the automations?</span><span class="accordion_toggle_icon u-transition-ease"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" class="icon"><path d="M15.9174 9L12.0496 12.9344L8.08264 9L6 9L12.0496 15L18 9L15.9174 9Z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg></span></button></h3><div data-accordion="content-wrap" class="accordion_content_wrap"><div class="accordion_content_padding"><div class="accordion_content_text u-rich-text u-color-faded w-richtext"><p>No coding needed! Our automation builder is intuitive with options for every skill level — from Quick Automations that guide you step-by-step to our customizable Flow Builder where you can create simple or robust solutions. Plus, our AI can help build flows for you automatically. 👀</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div data-wf--spacer--section-space="lg-180px" id="" class="u-section-spacer w-variant-60a7ad7d-02b0-6682-95a5-2218e6fd1490 u-ignore-trim"></div><div class="u-cover-absolute u-hide-if-empty"></div><div class="u-embed-js w-embed w-script"><script>
	document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
	document.querySelectorAll("[data-accordion='wrap']").forEach((component, listIndex) => {
	if (component.dataset.scriptInitialized) return;
	component.dataset.scriptInitialized = "true";
	
	const closePrevious = component.getAttribute("data-close-previous") !== "false";
	const closeOnSecondClick = component.getAttribute("data-close-on-second-click") !== "false";
	const openOnHover = component.getAttribute("data-open-on-hover") === "true";
	const openByDefault = component.getAttribute("data-open-by-default") !== null && !isNaN(+component.getAttribute("data-open-by-default")) ? +component.getAttribute("data-open-by-default") : false;
	const list = component.querySelector("[data-accordion='list'");
	const rootStyles = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
	const ease = "power3.inOut";
	let previousIndex = null,
	closeFunctions = [];
	
	function flattenDisplayContents(slot) {
	if (!slot) return;
	let child = slot.firstElementChild;
	while (child && child.classList.contains("u-display-contents")) {
	while (child.firstChild) {
	slot.insertBefore(child.firstChild, child);
	}
	slot.removeChild(child);
	child = slot.firstElementChild;
	}
	}
	flattenDisplayContents(list);
	
	function removeCMSList(slot) {
	const dynList = Array.from(slot.children).find((child) => child.classList.contains("w-dyn-list"));
	if (!dynList) return;
	const nestedItems = dynList?.firstElementChild?.children;
	if (!nestedItems) return;
	const staticWrapper = [...slot.children];
	[...nestedItems].forEach(el => { const c = [...el.children].find(c => !c.classList.contains('w-condition-invisible')); c && slot.appendChild(c); });
	staticWrapper.forEach((el) => el.remove());
	}
	removeCMSList(list);
	
	component.querySelectorAll("[data-accordion='component']").forEach((card, cardIndex) => {
	const button = card.querySelector("[data-accordion='toggle-button']");
	const content = card.querySelector("[data-accordion='content-wrap']");
	
	if (!button || !content) return console.warn("Missing elements:", card);
	
	button.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
	button.setAttribute("id", "accordion_button_" + listIndex + "_" + cardIndex);
	content.setAttribute("id", "accordion_content_" + listIndex + "_" + cardIndex);
	button.setAttribute("aria-controls", content.id);
	content.setAttribute("aria-labelledby", button.id);
	content.style.display = "none";
	
	const refresh = () => {
	tl.invalidate();
	if (typeof ScrollTrigger !== "undefined") ScrollTrigger.refresh();
	};
	const tl = gsap.timeline({ paused: true, defaults: { duration: 0.45, ease: ease }, onComplete: refresh, onReverseComplete: refresh });
	tl.set(content, { display: "block" });
	tl.fromTo(content, { height: 0 }, { height: "auto" });
	
	const closeAccordion = () => card.classList.contains("is-active") && (card.classList.remove("is-active"), tl.reverse(), button.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false"));
	closeFunctions[cardIndex] = closeAccordion;
	
	const openAccordion = (instant = false) => {
	if (closePrevious && previousIndex !== null && previousIndex !== cardIndex) closeFunctions[previousIndex]?.();
	previousIndex = cardIndex;
	button.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
	card.classList.add("is-active");
	instant ? tl.progress(1) : tl.play();
	};
	if (openByDefault === cardIndex + 1) openAccordion(true);
	
	button.addEventListener("click", () => (card.classList.contains("is-active") && closeOnSecondClick ? (closeAccordion(), (previousIndex = null)) : openAccordion()));
	if (openOnHover) button.addEventListener("mouseenter", () => openAccordion());
	});
	});
	});
	</script></div></section></main><section class="footer_wrap u-background-1"><div data-wf--spacer--section-space="even" id="" class="u-section-spacer w-variant-41fc0c0a-cac3-53c9-9802-6a916e3fb342 	<script>
	if (!window.__mcEventTrackingBound) {
	window.__mcEventTrackingBound = true;
	
	// Strict migration alias:
	// fire old event when new signup event is clicked
	const eventAlias = {
	signup_cta_click: ['main_cta_click'],
	};
	
	const sendWfEvent = (name) => {
	if (window.wf?.sendEvent) {
	window.wf.sendEvent(name);
	} else if (window.wf?.ready) {
	window.wf.ready(function () {
	window.wf.sendEvent(name);
	});
	}
	};
	
	document.body.addEventListener(
	'click',
	(e) => {
	const target = e.target.closest('[data-event-name][data-cta-id], [eventName][ctaID]');
	if (!target) return;
	
	const eventName = target.dataset.eventName || target.getAttribute('eventName');
	const ctaId = target.dataset.ctaId || target.getAttribute('ctaID');
	
	if (!eventName || !ctaId) return;
	
	const eventsToSend = [eventName, ...(eventAlias[eventName] || [])].filter(Boolean);
	
	// de-dup just in case
	const uniqueEvents = [...new Set(eventsToSend)];
	
	uniqueEvents.forEach((name) => {
	window.gtag?.('event', name, {
	event_category: 'Button Click',
	event_label: 'Click Event',
	ctaID: ctaId,
	});
	
	window.clarity?.('event', name);
	sendWfEvent(name);
	});
	},
	{ capture: true },
	);
	}
	</script>
	
	<!-- Videos autoplay and off-screen optimization -->
	
	<script>
	document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
	const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
	(entries) => {
	entries.forEach((entry) => {
	const video = entry.target;
	
	if (entry.isIntersecting) {
	video.play().catch(() => {});
	} else {
	video.pause();
	}
	});
	},
	{
	threshold: 0.25, // 25% visible
	},
	);
	
	document.querySelectorAll('video').forEach((video) => {
	video.muted = true;
	video.playsInline = true;
	video.autoplay = true;
	
	observer.observe(video);
	});
	});
	</script><script type="module" src="https://static.cloudflareinsights.com/beacon.min.js/v4513226cdae34746b4dedf0b4dfa099e1781791509496" integrity="sha512-ZE9pZaUXND66v380QUtch/5sE9tPFh2zg45pR2PB0CVkCtOREv2AJKkSidISWkysEuQ0EH8faUU5du78bx87UQ==" data-cf-beacon='{"version":"2024.11.0","token":"28eaa43a0469495c9e930749046f86b8"}' crossorigin="anonymous"></script>
